## Einstein teaching at MIT with Hindi joke text

> Sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A realistic, slightly cinematic scene of Albert Einstein teaching a class at MIT in the early 20th century. Show him at a large chalkboard covered with equations, chalk in hand, mid‑explanation. The classroom has wooden desks, warm natural light from tall windows, and attentive students. On the chalkboard, clearly written in Devanagari, include the sentence: "मैं आपसे रिलेट नहीं कर पा रहा, क्योंकि रिलेटिविटी अभी तक डिस्कवर नहीं हुई है". Capture Einstein’s characteristic hair, suit, and expressive posture. Blend historical accuracy with playful anachronism, maintaining a high‑detail, academic visual style.
```

## Cutaway infographic of a modern jet airliner

> Minimal, fütüristik bilgi grafiği üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a highly technological 3D cutaway infographic designed to show the internal structure and operating principles of a modern commercial jet airliner. Present the aircraft body (including fuselage, wings, tail, and especially the turbofan engines) in a semi-disassembled cutaway manner to reveal its complex internal structure. Key components (for example: cockpit avionics system, cabin frame, cargo hold, fuel tanks, wing ribs/wing spars, engine compressors/turbines, landing gear mechanisms, etc.) should be neatly disassembled and arranged in a floating layout, with leader lines connecting them back to the main body. Each major part should have clear, professional English labels, stating the structural name and a brief functional description. The overall layout should be extremely clean and logical, with a simple background and a style resembling a futuristic interactive high-end engineering blueprint.
```

## Illustration based on long classical Chinese poem

> Afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Illustrate the following classical poem and include the full original text in the image.
The emperor of Han prized beauty and longed for a peerless consort, ruling the realm for many years without finding one. In the Yang family there was a girl who had just grown up, raised deep within the boudoir, unknown to the world. Born with natural beauty that could not be concealed, one day she was chosen to stand by the emperor’s side. A single backward glance and a smile brought forth a hundred charms; all the powdered faces of the six palaces lost their color.
[Full poem continues unchanged in Chinese script as provided above.]
```

## 90s-style film portrait from a reference photo

> Minimal, retro afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a 90s-style portrait of the same man as in the reference photo, keeping his exact facial features and expression. The image should look as if it was taken with a 1990s film camera, slightly grainy with warm tones. The scene takes place in a cozy, dimly lit room where a camera flash is clearly visible, casting focused light on the man while the background remains slightly shadowed.
Only the upper half of his body is visible in the frame. He is posing elegantly, wearing a simple yet stylish 90s outfit such as a casual shirt or turtleneck that complements the warm indoor setting.
The background features a white wall with a few magazine clippings and posters of Hollywood stars from the 90s pinned to it, adding a nostalgic touch. The overall mood should evoke a classic retro portrait look, natural yet artistically lit.

She holds up a huge puzzle piece (top-left piece, with the number "20" on it) with both hands, moving it towards the center of the frame. Her eyes are focused on the central puzzle area, her expression is serious, with a slight smile. The background features navy stripes, an anchor, and the text "Set Sail for the New Year". Top-right panel (Cherry Blossom Pink background): The same woman wears a pink lace dress, a pearl necklace, a princess hairstyle with a pink rose hairpin and crystal earrings. Bottom-left panel (Mint Green background): The same woman wears a mint green cotton and linen dress, in an artistic style, with natural long hair, a green hairband, and wooden earrings. She holds up the bottom-left puzzle piece (with the text "New Year's Day" on it) with both hands, moving it upwards to connect with the top-left piece. Her eyes look at the puzzle, her expression is serious, and her mouth is slightly pursed. The background features green plants, the text "Hope Grows", new sprouts, and leaves. Bottom-right panel (Lemon Yellow background): The same woman wears a yellow dress with a sunflower pattern, pigtails with yellow bows. She pushes in the last bottom-right puzzle piece (with the text "Happy" on it) to complete the puzzle. The four pieces perfectly form the complete pattern "2026 New Year's Day Happy" in the center of the frame. ## Edo-period Ukiyo-e reinterpretation of a modern scene

> Illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A Japanese Edo-period Ukiyo-e woodblock print. The overall feeling is a surreal collaboration between masters like Hokusai and Hiroshige, reimagining modern technology through an ancient lens.

**The scene:** {argument name="modern scene" default="a busy Shibuya scramble crossing"}

**Edo transformation logic:**
Characters wear Edo-era kimono but perform modern actions. All technology is transformed into surreal Edo equivalents:
* Smartphones are glowing, illustrated paper scrolls being read intently.
* Metro stations and trains are giant articulated wooden centipede carriages shuffling through crowds.
* Skyscrapers are reimagined as endless, towering wooden pagodas reaching into dramatic clouds.
* Robots and mecha appear as giant, armored woodblock golems.

The composition uses a flattened perspective with large, bold, hand-carved ink outlines. The background features heavily stylized Ukiyo-e wave patterns and dramatic, swirling clouds, with a distant Mt. Fuji visible on the horizon.

The image must look like a physical print, not a digital painting.
* Texture: strong visible wood grain texture and rough paper fibers throughout the piece.
* Printing imperfections: pigment bleeding is evident. Simulate hand-pressed plates with slight color misalignment for authenticity.
* Color palette: strictly limited to traditional mineral pigments, with dominant use of Prussian blue, vermilion red, and muted yellow ochre.
* Lighting: soft, flat, shadow-free lighting with no digital gradients.

Aspect ratio is 3:4 vertical poster. Include vertical Japanese calligraphy describing the scene and a traditional red artist seal stamp in a corner.
```

## Hong Kong Wedding Snap Prompt

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
An illustration of a wedding on the streets of Hong Kong — a candid in-between moment, not a posed key visual.

[PEOPLE]
Use the uploaded character image(s) as reference. Keep each character's hair, eyes and face exactly. Match the art style of the upload and apply it to the whole picture — people, buildings, vehicles, signage. Same linework, same shading, same colour. Never photographic.

[STYLING]
Bride — black cat-eye sunglasses, white tiered ruffle organza dress with sheer puff sleeves, a short tulle veil lifting in the wind, chunky black boots. Laughing. She holds a loose bunch of long-stem magenta lilies.
Groom — black rectangular sunglasses, dark brown leather jacket, black wide-leg trousers, black boots. Not smiling.

[SCENE]
They cross a downtown intersection hand in hand, the groom half a step ahead, her veil blown sideways. Behind them: pink, mint and yellow tong lau blocks, window grilles, aircon units, Chinese shop signs, tram wires. A red taxi passes. Thick yellow road markings.

[LOOK]
Harsh early-afternoon sun. Lit and shadowed areas split with a crisp edge — no soft even glow, no airbrushed gradients. Draw the background in full detail, crisp all the way back, no blur or haze.
Keep them small and off-centre, and leave the street messy: passersby, scooters, shop signs, hanging laundry.
Vertical 3:4, no watermark. A drawing, not a photograph.

[LETTERING]
Across the lower third, centred: "{argument name="text input" default="Text Input"}" in a thin, wide-tracked white sans-serif, spelled exactly. Below it two centred lines of very small white serif text. Flat and clean, no shadow or box.
```

## Retro Red Telephone Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

Preserve the woman’s exact natural facial identity and recognizable features from the reference—same face shape, eyes, eyebrows, nose, lips, jawline, skin tone, facial proportions, and overall likeness. Do not reshape, beautify, age, or alter her identity.

Place her in a dramatic monochromatic deep-red interior with a luxurious vintage atmosphere. She is seated confidently on a dark-red sofa beside a matching red table, holding a classic glossy red vintage telephone receiver beside her face. The matching rotary telephone sits clearly on the table, connected by a curled red cord.

She wears a sophisticated oversized burgundy-red tailored coat with broad lapels and a black fitted top underneath. Add a plain burgundy baseball cap with absolutely no logo, lettering, symbols, or text, paired with sleek oversized black rectangular sunglasses. Her dark hair is a smooth shoulder-length bob visible beneath the cap.

Her expression is poised and mysterious, with relaxed lips and confident body language. One hand holds the telephone receiver naturally while the other rests elegantly near her body.

## 90s Streetwear Editorial Portrait

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A {argument name="subject" default="young male model"} is captured mid-stride with a confident, energetic swagger in a 90s minimalist streetwear editorial style. His slicked-back hair features a distinct wet look with subtle highlights catching the texture, while narrow, dark rectangular 90s sunglasses obscure his eyes, which gaze slightly to the viewer's left beneath neutral eyebrows. His mouth is caught slightly open with relaxed lips, suggesting mid-speech or purposeful motion. He wears a {argument name="clothing" default="heavy, long black wool overcoat over a starkly contrasting crisp white t-shirt"}. His body is angled slightly to his right with his weight shifted forward, shoulders hunched inward as both hands, clad in matte black gloves, are raised symmetrically to his upper chest. His hands tense with engaged muscles, tightly gripping and adjusting the thick fabric of the overcoat's wide collar. He walks along a light concrete grey sidewalk that transitions into a dark grey asphalt road in the foreground, where slightly faded, worn painted double yellow parallel lines ground the bottom right of the frame. The background falls into a medium spatial depth, slightly blurred to isolate the subject in an asymmetric composition. To the right, a {argument name="background" default="weathered, exposed brick building exterior"} dominates, characterized by dark warm brown and deep red tones with charcoal variations, a rough matte finish, patchy soot weathering, uneven brick alignment, and prominent dark mortar lines. To the left, a worn metal-framed doorway creates a deep, shadow-filled recess. The scene is bathed in a cool, moody, and mysterious atmosphere under natural overcast outdoor lighting acting as a large diffused softbox from above and the front. Subtle highlights graze his cheekbones and forehead, while short, gray, soft-edged shadows fall beneath his chin, under the thick collar, and cast gently onto the concrete. The split-complementary color palette relies on desaturated cool neutral greys and stark blacks and whites, contrasting against the warm earthy red-browns of the background masonry. Shot on a 50mm lens at f/2.8, 1/500s, and ISO 400, this candid digital street fashion photograph embraces a cinematic Matrix aesthetic with a slightly soft focus, medium depth of field, straight-on perspective, and visible digital noise grain, finalized with cooled shadow tones and lifted blacks for a flat matte finish that preserves the dynamic range in the dark coat, all beautifully framed in a 4:5 vertical format.
```

Analog film grain, muted greens, pastel pink dominance. NO NEON.",

## Elegant Men's Twilight Editorial

> Retro, lüks mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A young, conventionally attractive male model stands confidently in the center of the frame, positioned straight on to the camera with level shoulders and a deliberately posed demeanor. His meticulously styled hair complements a chiseled jawline and a closed, neutral mouth with relaxed eyebrows, while his eyes look off-camera to the left with a sophisticated, unsmiling expression. He wears a tailored, {argument name="blazer color" default="deep navy blue"} double-breasted blazer adorned with prominent, warm gold buttons, layered over a crisp white dress shirt with a patterned navy tie and a sharp white pocket square, paired with {argument name="trouser color" default="crisp cream"} trousers. Both of his relaxed hands rest inside the front pockets of the cream trousers, gently pulling the blazer fabric slightly backward. He stands on a solid expanse of warm beige and brown loose gravel within an upscale outdoor courtyard at twilight. The background falls into a medium depth of field with a beautifully blurred, deep spatial arrangement. In the left midground sits a thriving, rounded topiary bush in a dark classical planter subtly illuminated by ground-level pathway lights, while the left background features an aged, rough matte dark stone building with a paned glass arched window emitting a warm interior light. To the right midground, dense, dark olive green sculpted hedges form a structural boundary next to a vintage black iron streetlamp casting an amber glow from its dual bulbs in a glass housing, all framing a large leafy tree situated directly behind the subject in the center background. The scene exudes an elegant, wealthy aesthetic bathed in a {argument name="lighting style" default="moody, high-contrast twilight"} atmosphere. A 45-degree front-left softbox key light wraps smoothly around his features, preserving sharp specular highlights on the bridge of his nose, the pristine white shirt collar, and the gold blazer buttons, while casting short, deep black shadows with soft gradual edges on the right side of his face, beneath his jawline, and inside the structural folds of the navy blazer. The overall color palette balances complementary tones of deep navy, cream, warm gold, and dark forest green under a golden ambient fill, mixing a 5500K daylight balance on the subject with exceptionally warm 3200K practical background lights. Shot on an 85mm lens at f/2.8 for a tack-sharp focal point on his brightly lit face that draws the eye down the contrasting lapels before drifting to the out-of-focus creamy bokeh of the practical lights, the composition evokes a classic menswear editorial campaign. The highly detailed, photorealistic digital image features a Kodak Portra 400 aesthetic, processed with a 1/125s shutter speed and ISO 400, completed by subtle film grain, slightly crushed shadows, warm and teal split toning with deep navy shadows and amber highlights, natural skin texture preservation, and enhanced clarity on the tailored fabrics, all naturally framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

```
Vertical studio portrait in a 3:4 format. The frame is shot approximately from the waist up, with a slight camera tilt or dynamic diagonal composition. The main subject is positioned in the center, slightly shifted to the right, with his body subtly turned toward the viewer. The background is a {argument name="background" default="solid, saturated red"} with a grainy texture, creating the feeling of a vintage magazine cover or music poster. Main Subject: Use the person from the attached reference as the main character. Accurately reproduce his facial features and identity. Preserve his distinctive look: mysterious, confident, slightly detached expression, with his gaze partially concealed by narrow dark sunglasses. Pose: The subject stands in a relaxed yet expressive pose. His head is tilted slightly forward and to the side, as if he is intently listening during a phone conversation. With one hand, he holds a telephone receiver to his ear; with the other, he holds the other part of the telephone. The pose should feel effortless, fashionable, and cinematic. Clothing & Accessories: The subject wears a {argument name="outfit" default="black oversized blazer"} or jacket with a deep neckline, worn over bare skin or with no visible shirt underneath. The look is minimalist and dark, contrasting sharply against the vivid red background. He wears narrow black vintage-style sunglasses. In his hand is a {argument name="accessory" default="wired telephone receiver"} with a coiled cord. Additional Details: A visible coiled telephone cord connects the receiver to the device. The subject's hair is dark messy with well groomed beard. The lighting emphasizes his face, lips, and part of his neck, while the clothing nearly merges into the deep shadows. The red background should be rich and slightly rough, with vintage film noise. Photography Style: Fashion editorial photography Inspired by the 1980s-1990s, with the aesthetic of a glossy magazine, music album cover, or art poster. High contrast, deep shadows, pronounced grain, and a subtle film texture. Visual style: retro-futurism, fashion portrait, analog photography, dramatic studio lighting. Atmosphere: The atmosphere should feel mysterious, bold, and stylish. The image should evoke the feeling of nighttime urban culture, music, fashion, and a secretive phone conversation. The subject should appear charismatic, confident, slightly dangerous, and cinematic. Image Quality: Highly detailed image with pronounced film grain, deep shadows, and a vivid red background. Sharp focus on the face, sunglasses, hands, and retro telephone device. Preserve the vintage texture while maintaining high image quality, strong visual impact, and a polished fashion/editorial aesthetic
```

## Futuristic Retro Fashion Editorial

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal arayüz tasarımı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Design a square 1:1 visual artwork for {argument name="brand name" default="[BRAND NAME]"} that feels like an experimental pop-fashion photograph from a futuristic retro archive.

Let {argument name="hero color" default="[HERO COLOR]"} dominate the scene, but avoid a simple flat backdrop. Create a layered studio environment using tonal gradients, soft cast shadows, floating geometric fragments, and subtle paper-print imperfections.

The main subject is a single adult fashion model styled in a distinctive look that complements {argument name="brand name" default="[BRAND NAME]"}. Capture an energetic candid moment, with the subject turning toward a handheld retro digital camera and photographing something outside the frame.

Make the camera an important storytelling element rather than a decorative prop. Show its small display clearly enough to suggest a captured image, while keeping the scene photorealistic.

Add expressive white (#FFFFFF) hand-drawn artwork throughout the scene: loose geometric sketches, tiny stars, camera-interface-inspired symbols, sweeping lines, imperfect circles, and abstract gestures. Integrate these marks into the environment at different depths so some appear behind the subject while others float around the foreground.

Give the image a late-1990s/early-2000s experimental magazine mood with contemporary fashion photography, crisp details, subtle analog grain, realistic skin texture, dimensional lighting, and energetic editorial framing.

Use approximately 5500K neutral studio lighting. Maintain strong separation between photographic elements and graphic artwork while making them feel intentionally designed as one composition.

Absolutely no readable text, typography, slogans, captions, logos, watermarks, or written brand names.

The final result should resemble a rare experimental fashion editorial discovered in a futuristic design archive—confident, playful, tactile, unconventional, and visually memorable.
```

## Monochrome Carbon Dust Art Style

> Afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Transform the image into an ultra-high-contrast monochrome dust-particle artwork.

Render the entire image using millions of tiny {argument name="particle types" default="black toner particles, carbon dust, xerox powder, ink speckles, and stochastic grain"}. Every object, person, vehicle, shadow, and texture must be reconstructed entirely from particle density rather than photographic shading.

Aggressively destroy photographic detail. Faces become simplified silhouettes and abstract particle masses. Clothing folds, vehicle reflections, windows, and environmental details dissolve into dense dust clusters. Large portions of the image disappear into pure white negative space.

Use extreme information loss, photocopy degradation, toner scatter, print erosion, xerox artifacting, and particle abstraction. Dark areas become dense black dust masses. Midtones become sparse particle fields. Highlights vanish completely into white.

The image should feel like a {argument name="aesthetic" default="photocopy of a photocopy of a photocopy, printed with black toner on white paper"}. Raw underground streetwear aesthetic, underground car culture poster, DIY punk zine, archive fashion graphic design, bootleg magazine artwork.

Massive white negative space. Floating isolated subjects. Broken contours. Fading edges. Subjects emerging from particle density. Toner-built forms. Dust-built silhouettes. Scanned print imperfections. Carbon particle rendering. Stochastic grain shading.

The final image must look as if it was created entirely from black dust particles scattered across white paper, not a photograph with a filter applied.

NEGATIVE PROMPT:

clean photography, realistic photo, detailed faces, realistic skin texture, smooth gradients, cinematic lighting, HDR, glossy surfaces, realistic reflections, painterly artwork, digital painting, 3D render, CGI, sharp details, clean edges, modern graphic design, vector art, comic halftone dots, newspaper halftone dots, color, grayscale gradients, high-detail realism, polished commercial photography, realistic backgrounds, environmental depth, perfect image quality, beauty retouching, hyperrealism, crisp details, professional studio photography
```

## Human Fragments Editorial Poster

> Afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a 9:16 editorial poster in the HUMAN FRAGMENTS visual system.

Theme: [{argument name="theme" default="football / badminton / jump rope / floral design"}]
Subject: [person, age, gender, appearance]
Action: [specific movement or task]
Scene: [location and environment]
Title: [{argument name="title" default="custom two-word title"}]
Supporting line: [{argument name="subtitle" default="one short phrase"}]
Year: 2026

Use oversized cropped typography as the main composition structure. The letters should act like windows that reveal the surrounding scene, such as the sky, court, buildings, flowers, studio, or ground.

Keep the main person visually complete in front of the typography. Do not let the letters cut through the face, torso, thighs, or other important body parts.

Let one key action element break beyond the letterforms to create depth:
- a football and shoe close to the camera
- a badminton racket and shuttlecock
- a jump rope forming a large arc
- a bouquet, stems, leaves, or hands extending across the type

The person, typography, and environment must feel like one continuous composition, not a photo placed beside typography.
```

Render using a limited retro duotone palette consisting of {argument name="base colors" default="warm cream (#F2DFC0), muted ivory, charcoal navy (#243647), slate gray, cool gray"}, and {argument name="accent color" default="vivid burnt orange (#F57A2A)"} as the only accent color. Preserve the exact color harmony with high-contrast flat graphic shadows and subtle tonal layering.
Create clean, simplified geometric shadow planes with irregular angular shapes, while retaining loose hand-drawn imperfections. Outlines feature expressive pressure-sensitive thick-and-thin strokes, alternating between bold contour lines and delicate hairline sketch marks. Avoid perfectly uniform vector paths; every line should feel naturally drawn by hand with slight wobble, tapering ends, and visible stroke rhythm.
Facial features are modeled using layered graphic shapes mixed with spontaneous sketch lines, giving a balance between realism and illustration. Hair and beard are built from hundreds of energetic flowing strokes, overlapping curved ink-like marks, loose scribbles, tapered strands, and textured clusters, maintaining an organic handcrafted appearance rather than smooth digital rendering.
Use minimal yet intentional cross-contour marks, subtle scratch textures, tiny construction lines, and scattered expressive sketch strokes throughout the face and clothing. Shadows are composed of flat color blocks with occasional textured edges and thin internal contour lines to preserve depth without photorealistic gradients.
Background is a solid {argument name="background color" default="warm cream"} with no scenery, no texture except extremely subtle paper grain. Overall style resembles a premium contemporary graphic portrait poster, blending semi-vector precision with expressive ink drawing, bold editorial composition, handcrafted imperfections, graphic shadow design, organic line variation, and crisp high-resolution illustration.
Negative style: photorealism, glossy rendering, airbrush gradients, watercolor, oil paint, 3D rendering, comic halftone dots, overly smooth vector curves, excessive texture, neon colors, saturated rainbow palette, blurry edges, uniform line weight, flat lifeless strokes.
```

## Haute Couture Storm Editorial

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra realistic high-fashion editorial photography using the uploaded reference image as an ABSOLUTE 1:1 reference for the couture gown, storm atmosphere and fashion aesthetic.

Use the uploaded identity photo ONLY for:
- exact face identity
- exact facial anatomy
- exact eyes
- exact eyebrows
- exact nose
- exact lips
- exact natural skin tone

REFERENCE CONTROLS EVERYTHING ELSE.

Canon EOS R5.

85mm lens.

RAW photograph.

CAMERA

Very wide cinematic shot.

The camera is positioned very far away.

The woman appears small in the landscape, occupying only about 15–20% of the frame.

She is running across the top of a grassy hill.

The dramatic landscape dominates the composition.

FULL BODY.

The entire floor-length gown is completely visible.

Nothing is cropped.

POSE

The woman is running naturally across the hill.

She is viewed mostly from the side with a slight three-quarter angle.

She is NOT looking at the camera.

She looks forward toward the direction she is running.

One hand tightly holds the front of the gown across her chest to keep it from blowing open.

The other hand gathers part of the skirt while running.

Her posture shows urgency as she struggles against the violent wind.

The running motion is natural, elegant and realistic.

HAIR

Hair is styled into a sleek elegant low bun.

Only a few loose strands escape and whip violently in the wind.

STRICT DRESS LOCK

Recreate EXACTLY the uploaded couture gown.

Keep identical:

- floor-length ivory haute couture gown
- oversized balloon sleeves
- deep plunging V neckline
- fitted gathered waist
- enormous multi-layered skirt
- identical silhouette
- identical proportions
- identical fabric
- identical stitching
- identical folds
- identical volume

The gown MUST remain floor-length.

The skirt must touch the ground exactly like the reference.

Do NOT redesign the dress.

Do NOT shorten the skirt.

Do NOT change the sleeves.

Do NOT change the neckline.

Keep the jewelry identical.

WIND

Violent storm wind.

The enormous skirt flies dramatically in multiple directions.

Huge sculptural folds sweep left, right and backward.

The long train streams several meters behind her while she runs.

Every fabric layer moves independently with realistic couture fabric physics.

The oversized sleeves inflate dramatically.

The movement feels powerful but physically believable.

BACKGROUND

Wide grassy hill.

Massive black storm clouds cover the sky.

A huge bright lightning bolt strikes in the distance behind the woman, illuminating part of the clouds.

Additional faint lightning flashes are visible deeper inside the storm.

No buildings.

No people.

Only the dramatic landscape.

LIGHTING

Dark storm atmosphere.

Soft diffused overcast light.

Brief cold flashes from the lightning illuminate parts of the clouds and subtly rim-light the flowing gown.

Luxury Vogue editorial lighting.

REALISM

Visible skin texture.

Correct anatomy.

Natural running motion.
```

## Neo-Constructivist Typographic Brand Poster

> Minimal afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A bold Neo-Constructivist typographic brand advertisement poster for “{argument name="brand name" default="BRAND NAME"}”. The composition is a full-bleed, portrait-format graphic design piece that fuses extreme typographic-as-architecture with 2 to 3 iconic hero products of {argument name="brand name" default="BRAND NAME"} embedded directly into the layout.
The letters of “{argument name="brand name" default="BRAND NAME"}” are massively oversized, fragmented, and cropped at the edges of the frame, functioning as giant abstract geometric building blocks. The letterforms are modular, maze-like, and interlocking — constructed from rounded rectangles, angular channels, and tight negative-space gaps. The entire composition is a strict grid-structured layout divided into rectangular zones.
HERO PRODUCTS: 2 to 3 of {argument name="brand name" default="BRAND NAME"}’s most iconic, recognizable products are placed within dedicated rectangular grid zones inside the composition. The products are rendered in a flat, high-contrast, graphic illustration style — NOT photographic. They are silhouetted, simplified into bold flat shapes, and colored using only the brand’s official color palette. The products sit inside clearly defined rectangular or square cells within the grid, framed by the surrounding typographic mass. Each product occupies its own zone — one product per cell — with clean hard edges. The products feel architectural and structural, as if they are part of the typographic grid itself, not floating decorations. Examples: for Nike — a side-profile Air Max sneaker silhouette, a jersey, and a sports bag; for Coca-Cola — a glass bottle, a can, and a glass with ice; for Apple — an iPhone, MacBook, and AirPods; for McDonald’s — a burger, fries box, and a drink cup.
The products are rendered WITHOUT any background within their cell — just a flat solid brand color fill behind each product silhouette, so they integrate cleanly into the poster’s color system. Product details are reduced to essential bold outlines and flat fill areas — no gradients, no shadows, no photorealism. They should look like vintage product icons or stamp illustrations.
Color palette is strictly limited to THREE colors from {argument name="brand name" default="BRAND NAME"}’s official brand identity: {argument name="primary color" default="PRIMARY COLOR"} for the dominant typographic mass and letterforms, {argument name="accent color" default="SECONDARY COLOR"} for product silhouettes and the single bold geometric accent shape (a perfect circle or bold stripe), and {argument name="background color" default="BACKGROUND COLOR"} as the deep solid background. All fills are 100% flat. No gradients, no tints, no textures on the products themselves.
Somewhere along the left or right vertical edge, the brand name “{argument name="brand name" default="BRAND NAME"}” is typeset in large, bold, condensed sans-serif capital letters rotated 90 degrees in the accent color, anchoring the composition as a vertical label. At least one perfect geometric circle in the accent color appears in the upper zone of the composition as a structural element.
The overall aesthetic references Soviet Constructivism (Rodchenko, El Lissitzky), 1970s Swiss Internat
```

Use the uploaded reference images as the exact composition, styling, pose inspiration, facial framing, {argument name="color style" default="monochrome"} aesthetic, and cinematic atmosphere reference. The overall artwork should feel like a luxury fashion editorial poster mixed with a moody urban action-drama aesthetic. Center Frame:
A full-body portrait of the man standing confidently in the center wearing an {argument name="clothing" default="all-black outfit"} — fitted black button-up shirt with rolled sleeves, slim black trousers, luxury black wristwatch, chain necklace, and white sneakers. One hand is inside the pocket while the other adjusts the collar or touches the neck. Pose should feel confident, calm, dominant, and cinematic.

Side Portraits:

* one looking away thoughtfully with soft dramatic lighting,
* one wearing black sunglasses with a stylish confident attitude,
* one side-profile portrait with strong jawline emphasis,
* one contemplative pose with hand near face.

Add soft glowing rim lights, smoky overlays, and dramatic shadows throughout the collage. Include faint city lights, moody grayscale contrast, reflective wet floor textures, and layered depth effects.

Color grading should be primarily monochrome black-and-white with deep blacks, silver highlights, matte shadows, and subtle warm-gray tones. Maintain ultra-realistic skin texture and high dynamic range lighting.

Lighting:
Use dramatic cinematic lighting with strong contrast, soft edge lighting, volumetric fog, shadow gradients, and luxury editorial highlights. Faces should have sharp detail with soft diffused glow around hair and shoulders.

Style:
ultra-realistic, cinematic monochrome portrait collage, luxury fashion editorial, dark urban aesthetic, high-end poster design, realistic male model photography, moody atmosphere, smoky cinematic composition, dramatic shadows, hyper-detailed skin texture, realis

## Dark Luxury Fallen Angel Editorial

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-photorealistic 2x2 luxury glossy editorial collage featuring the same woman across all four panels, with strict identity consistency, strict body consistency, the same skin tone, the same proportions, and the same elegant posture language in every frame. CONCEPT: A dark luxury fashion editorial centered on sculptural back lines, shoulders, neck, arms, and an open-back deep red dress, with a mysterious fallen-angel aura. The mood is sensual, elegant, expensive, cinematic, controlled, and minimal. The overall feeling should be high-fashion editorial, refined and dramatic, never cheap or costume-like. SUBJECT: A beautiful woman with long loose black hair, consistent identity across all four panels. Hair is sleek yet softly textured, flowing naturally down the back and shoulders. Makeup is dark smoky eye makeup with softly diffused black shadow, defined brows, and a polished editorial finish. Nails are glossy deep red or black-cherry, long and elegant. Expression and body language are poised, feminine, and powerful. OUTFIT: Deep crimson / burgundy / blood-red velvet or satin halter dress with an open back, realistic glossy fabric highlights, natural draping, subtle tension around the waist and hips, and elegant luxury construction. The dress must look premium and believable, with no fabric glitches. The red should feel rich, dark, and sophisticated, not bright or cheap. WINGS: Large dark red angel wings behind the woman, dramatic and elegant, slightly torn and weathered for a fallen-angel feeling. The wings should feel sculptural and luxurious, not fantasy cosplay. Feather texture should be rich, layered, deep burgundy to oxblood red, with softly darkened damaged edges. The wings must integrate beautifully with the poses and lighting without overpowering the fashion editorial tone. SKIN: Glossy editorial wet-skin finish with a subtle oil sheen on the shoulders, upper back, neck, arms, and along the spine line. Real skin texture must remain visible, including pores, natural tonal variation, and realistic highlights. No plastic smoothing. The sheen should look expensive, controlled, and believable, not sweaty or messy. ENVIRONMENT: Minimal studio background with a warm grey, taupe, or soft beige seamless backdrop. No props, no distractions, no clutter. LIGHTING: Premium glossy studio lighting: - large softbox key light from the upper side - narrow rim light tracing the shoulders, spine, and wing edges - soft warm highlights on skin and fabric - deep but clean shadows - smooth gradient falloff - high-end editorial contrast - no harsh flash - no fake glow GRID 2x2: TOP LEFT: Back-facing pose, arms raised above the head, hands resting in the hair. Spine line, shoulder blades, and upper wing structure are emphasized with glossy highlights. Black hair falls naturally over part of the back while the open-back red dress and wings remain clearly visible. TOP RIGHT: Slight torso twist, one shoulder closer to the camera, head turned subtly away. The red dress catches light along one side, and the dark red angel wings frame the silhouette elegantly. Strong emphasis on shoulder, waist, and back contour. BOTTOM LEFT: Closer crop on the upper back, neck, shoulders, hands, and partial wing texture. Focus on wet-skin texture, glossy nails, smoky makeup details near the eye area if visible, and luxury detail in the red dress fabric. BOTTOM RIGHT: Elegant half-turn pose from behind, body rotated slightly, side profile subtly hinted but face mostly hidden. Strong glossy rim light on the shoulder, waist curve, and the edge of the dark red wings. The silhouette should feel sleek, mysterious, and powerful. CAMERA: Medium format fashion photography look, 85mm or 100mm lens, f/2.0, ISO 100, shallow depth of field, ultra-sharp focus on skin texture, hair texture, feather detail, and fabric finish, ultra high resolution, luxury editorial quality. COLOR GRADE: Deep burgundy, oxblood, crimson, and soft warm neutrals, with subtle champagne skin highlights and elegant shadow contrast. Fine film grain. Rich dark editorial tonality. No oversaturation. REALISM RULES: Anatomically correct back, shoulders, arms, hands, neck, and spine curve. Realistic skin sheen. Realistic red dress tension and drape. Realistic feather texture and wing anatomy. No exaggerated body proportions. No fantasy illustration look. Keep everything photorealistic and luxury editorial. NEGATIVE PROMPT: plastic skin, over-smoothed skin, fake oil, fake glow, sweaty messy look, harsh flash, distorted spine, bad anatomy, extra fingers, warped hands, broken shoulders, unrealistic wings, cheap costume wings, fake feathers, fabric glitches, unrealistic dress, altered body proportions, cartoon, anime, illustration, text, watermark, logo, oversharpening, AI artifacts
```

## Cinematic Rain Night Portrait

> Sinematik, fütüristik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A mesmerizing, cinematic allure defines this striking night portrait, capturing an ethereal moment enveloped in falling rain. The subject stands with her eyes gently closed, her serene expression illuminated by a cool, blue-toned glow that catches the moisture glistening on her skin. Her sleek, platinum blonde hair falls straight down her shoulders, contrasting beautifully with the deep, dark tones of the night. She is adorned in a shimmering, deep-V black slip dress paired with a wet-look dark jacket slung casually off her shoulders, while a delicate silver necklace with a brilliant sapphire blue pendant rests against her collarbone. Water droplets streak down the frame, creating a dynamic texture over a softly blurred urban backdrop filled with distant, warm bokeh lights. The strong blue rim lighting sculpts her silhouette and reflects brilliantly off the wet surfaces, creating a moody, high-end noir aesthetic that feels lifted straight from a modern cyberpunk film or an elite fashion editorial.
```

He is dressed in striking winter formalwear, wearing a textured dark flat cap, a {argument name="coat type" default="heavy gray wool overcoat"} over a dark vest, a stark white dress shirt, and a diagonally striped gray tie. The expansive black canopy of the umbrella is heavily weighted to the right side of the composition, creating an asymmetric balance against his rigid posture while shielding him from a crisp, rainy evening. To the top right, a vintage classical building facade made of smooth matte plaster transitions through an aged gradient of warm terracotta and pale yellow, detailed with architectural columns and arched windows glowing softly from within. The atmosphere is dominated by a stark split-complementary palette of charcoal gray, warm amber, stark white, and deep black, entirely shaped by a highly directional, {argument name="lighting type" default="bright, cool-toned single-source hard light"} striking the subject directly from the front. This intrusive, raw flash creates dramatic, high-contrast illumination with specular highlights gleaming sharply on his forehead, the bridge of his nose, his cheeks, and the scattered rain droplets across the dark umbrella canopy. Harsh, deep black shadows with sharply defined edges are cast directly from the umbrella shaft onto his crisp white chest, from the thick overcoat lapels onto his dark vest, and fall heavily onto the unseen space behind him. Captured as a realistic digital photograph with tack-sharp focus on the subject and a shallow depth of field, the image embraces a paparazzi aesthetic and cinematic night photography influences, complete with visible digital noise and enhanced clarity on the intricate fabric textures. The straight-on composition mimics a DSLR equipped with a 50mm lens at f/2.8 and a fast shutter speed to sync with the bare-bulb on-camera flash, processed with increased contrast to emphasize the harsh flash falloff, a color grade that cools the foreground while warming the ambient background streetlights, and a slight edge-darkening vignette, all beautifully framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.

Magazine Layout

LYNTILLA

Quality

## High Speed Action Editorial

> Sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high fashion studio action shot designed with a cinematic, hyper dynamic editorial approach that captures movement, tension, and frozen energy in a single frame. The scene is set against a dark, solid seamless background that absorbs light and enhances contrast, creating a dramatic void-like environment that isolates the subject and emphasizes form, motion, and lighting precision.

The lighting setup is a hard strobe configuration, engineered to produce crisp, sculptural highlights and deep, defined shadows with razor sharp edges. Every contour of the model, fabric, and airborne particles is sharply defined through high intensity flash timing combined with a high shutter speed freeze, ensuring that even the fastest motion appears suspended in time with absolute clarity.

The camera is positioned at a {argument name="focal length" default="SUPER ultra wide 12mm"} focal length, exaggerating perspective and adding immersive distortion that enhances speed and impact. A side tracking angle is used, following the model’s motion laterally to intensify the feeling of acceleration and directional force. This perspective makes the scene feel as if the viewer is moving alongside the subject in real time.

The model is captured mid action, {argument name="action" default="lunging aggressively into a full sprint"} with powerful athletic energy. The body posture conveys urgency and momentum, with forward drive clearly visible in the stride, shoulders angled into motion, and core engaged. Hair whips violently backward due to rapid acceleration, forming fluid arcs that contrast with the sharp frozen lighting style.

In the foreground and motion path, {argument name="liquid effect" default="an iced coffee is captured mid spill, exploding into a long, elegant arc of liquid suspended in mid air"}. Droplets are individually visible, frozen at different distances from the cup, creating a sense of chaotic precision. Crushed ice fragments scatter outward, each piece sharply defined and glinting under the strobe light, enhancing texture and realism.

The composition emphasizes controlled chaos, balancing fashion editorial aesthetics with raw kinetic energy. The wardrobe is sleek and high fashion, designed to complement motion without cluttering the visual narrative. Fabric reacts naturally to speed, creating flowing trails and subtle tension folds.

Overall, the image blends luxury fashion photography with extreme motion capture techniques, resulting in a visually striking moment that feels both cinematic and impossible, as if time itself has been stopped at the peak of action.
```

Hyperrealistic 4K HDR fashion editorial photo of a young man with black-framed glasses, wearing a {argument name="hoodie" default="dark forest green oversized hoodie"}, a dark beanie hat, wide-leg baggy pants, and classic canvas sneakers — standing next to an {argument name="companion" default="anthropomorphic cartoon duck character"} dressed in a {argument name="jacket" default="gold bomber jacket"}, black joggers, gold chain medallion, and yellow high-top sneakers. Both striking a bold, arrogant, confident street-style pose. Urban environment with vibrant graffiti-covered concrete walls, spray-painted tags and throw-ups, neon orange and yellow dripping lettering, Japanese kanji murals splashed across the background. Boombox sitting on the ground beside them. Saturated punchy colors — electric yellow, deep forest green, neon orange, bold red accents. Graffiti text tags: "RAP", "MUSIC", "ART INFINITY", "OVA10", "EXIT", "HRN". Street fashion photography aesthetic mixed with urban illustration. Cinematic dramatic lighting, deep shadows, rim light. Ultra-sharp 4K HDR details, photorealistic textures, high contrast, fashion magazine quality shot.
```

## Bohemian Sequin Fashion Portrait

## Artistic Royal Purple Portrait

> Lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Edit this photo without changing face, turning it an artistic portrait. The male boy should be elegantly seated on the floor, wearing a {argument name="top color" default="royal purple"} top, {argument name="pants style" default="soft loose white jeans"}, and {argument name="footwear" default="chunky gray and white sneakers"}. Her facial expression is cute smile while looking upwards. The background should be an artistic monochrome (black and white) composition featuring a soft close-up side profile of the same face. Keep Face and body 100% match with reference image uploaded it’s very important.Watermark: At the bottom, in a lower left corner, in small, bold, italicized brown text, write “Dpixel” and add a shooting star.watermark spelling correction is very important.

## Pop Art Silhouette Splatter

> Illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
This image features a highly stylized silhouette profile of a young man with {argument name="hair style" default="tousled, dark hair"} and round glasses, set against a {argument name="background color" default="vibrant, saturated red"} background. The aesthetic leans heavily into a modern pop-art and street-art fusion, characterized by explosive, dynamic paint splatters in {argument name="splatter colors" default="electric purple and bright yellow"} that appear to trail off the subject’s head and shoulders, suggesting movement or a creative "burst" of thought.
​The lighting is dramatic and high-contrast, utilizing a chiaroscuro effect where the subject's face is largely cast in deep shadow, while sharp red highlights define the bridge of his nose, lips, and neck. The overall composition is sleek and graphic, reminiscent of a contemporary digital illustration or a stylized comic book cover, evoking a sense of moody introspection combined with an energetic, artistic flair.
```

## Editorial Magazine Cover Design

> Lüks manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
An editorial magazine cover for '{argument name="magazine title" default="Imaginary AURA"}', featuring a confident {argument name="ethnicity" default="South Asian"} woman as the central figure. She has flowing, wavy dark hair and is wearing a rich emerald-green velvet blazer over a matching silk turtleneck.
​Her look is completed with striking chunky gold hoop earrings and a heavy, layered gold chain link necklace. She has a strong, powerful expression, directly engaging the viewer with her warm, makeup-enhanced brown eyes.
​The background features a warm, sandy desert landscape at twilight. A vertical headline in a stylish gold font reads, 'VISIONARIES: THE CREATIVE CANVAS,' on the left. The bottom right features the text, 'DESERT BLOSSOM: Finding New Forms,' with the sub-headline 'A Journey Through Textures and Light.'
​This captivating cover captures the essence of artistic exploration and the beauty found within diverse cultural contexts.
```

## High-Energy Energy Drink Advertisement

> Sinematik, fütüristik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
This high-energy advertisement features a dynamic composition centered on a powerful female athlete in mid-leap, celebrating with a triumphant fist pump. She is dressed in neon green and dark forest green athletic wear that mirrors the branding of the "{argument name="brand name" default="Powey"}" energy drink. A large, tilted energy drink can dominates the midground, erupting with a dramatic splash of {argument name="liquid color" default="golden"} liquid, while crackling yellow lightning bolts surge around the subject to symbolize explosive energy. The background is a deep gradient green with the word "power" repeated in bold, oversized lime-green typography. The overall aesthetic is cinematic and high-contrast, utilizing a professional studio setup with a reflective dark floor and glowing neon accents. At the bottom, a smaller, upright can sits next to the bold sans-serif tagline "FUEL YOUR UNSTOPPABLE," grounding the vibrant, action-oriented scene.
```

## High-End Haute Couture Magazine Cover

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
This is a high-end fashion magazine cover for "{argument name="magazine name" default="GLAXY"}" featuring a young woman in a striking, {argument name="gown color" default="deep pink"} haute couture gown. The dress is crafted from a rich, heavy satin with a structured corset bodice and a high halter neck, accented by dramatic, oversized fabric roses on the shoulders that create a bold, sculptural silhouette. The model is captured in a confident, editorial pose with her hands on her hips, her {argument name="hair style" default="long wavy brown hair"} cascading over her shoulders.
​The composition utilizes soft, professional studio lighting that emphasizes the lustrous texture of the pink fabric against a minimalist, neutral cream background. Elegant serif typography in black and soft silver frames the model, featuring headlines like "GRACE & POWER: Modern Femininity Redefined" and "The Ultimate Lifestyle Issue." The overall aesthetic is one of cinematic luxury and sophisticated fashion photography, characteristic of a premium editorial publication.
```

## Minimalist Luxury Dessert Ad

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic commercial poster, minimalist studio setup with a smooth pastel background ({argument name="background color" default="soft lavender or mint green"}). Replace the model with the uploaded person’s face and features, keeping natural skin tone and realistic lighting. The subject stands confidently, slightly leaning against an {argument name="product" default="oversized luxury chocolate bar"} (or premium dessert like a layered cake or ice cream sandwich), holding a smaller version of the same product in hand with a subtle smile and relaxed pose. Outfit: {argument name="outfit style" default="clean aesthetic streetwear"} in coordinated tones matching the background, soft fabric textures, modern fit.

The product is hyper-detailed, glossy, and mouth-watering, with visible textures like melted chocolate, creamy layers, or crunchy toppings. Composition is balanced, with the product dominating the frame and the subject adding lifestyle appeal. Add large bold typography in the background with the brand name (customizable), partially behind the subject for depth. Include a short tagline at the bottom like “Pure indulgence, every bite.”

Lighting: soft studio lighting with gentle shadows and slight reflections on the floor for a premium ad feel. Depth of field slightly shallow for cinematic focus. 4K resolution, sharp focus, commercial food photography style, vibrant yet clean color grading, editorial advertising quality.
```
